Key generation, storage and HSMs. Key-rollover timing (RFC 6781, RFC 7583). Automated DS maintenance with CDS/CDNSKEY (RFC 7344, RFC 8078). Online signing and compact denial of existence (RFC 9824). Signing a zone yourself, for which the NSEC3 chain construction is in RFC 5155 §7.1.

## 16. Security

DNS runs over unauthenticated UDP, is used by everything, and answers anyone, so it gets attacked constantly. This section covers the attacks every implementation must defend against, and the concrete defences.

### 16.1 Cache poisoning

The goal is to make a resolver cache a false record (e.g. `www.bank.example A <attacker>`), which then misdirects every client of that resolver for the TTL.

**Off-path spoofing.** The attacker cannot see the resolver's query, but can send forged UDP responses that appear to come from the authoritative server. A forged response is accepted if it arrives before the real one and matches the destination port, the ID and the question (RFC 5452 §4).

**The Kaminsky attack (2008, CVE-2008-1447).** The old belief was "if `www.bank.example` is cached, the attacker must wait for the TTL to expire". Dan Kaminsky showed how to race again and again, without waiting:

1. Make the resolver look up a **random, non-existent** name: `r4nd0m1.bank.example`. It is never cached, so the resolver must ask.
2. Flood the resolver with forged responses for that query. They do not answer the question; they carry a *referral* or additional data: `bank.example NS ns.bank.example` and `ns.bank.example A <attacker>`. These are **in bailiwick**, so bailiwick checks alone do not stop them.
3. If one forgery wins the race (right ID), the resolver caches the attacker's server as authoritative for **all of** `bank.example`.
4. If it loses, try again at once with `r4nd0m2.bank.example`. At the time, many resolvers used a **fixed source port**, so only the 16-bit ID protected each race. That fell in seconds to minutes.

**Defences, in layers:**

| Defence | Effect | Reference |
|---|---|---|
| Random 16-bit ID from a CSPRNG | 2¹⁶ guesses per race | RFC 5452 §9.2 |
| **Random source port** per query | about another 2¹⁶ → about 2³² combined | RFC 5452 §9.2 |
| Strict response matching (address, ports, ID, question) | forgeries must match every field | RFC 5452 §9.1 |
| Bailiwick filtering and trust ranking; never overwrite answer data with glue | limits what a forgery can inject | RFC 5452 §6; RFC 2181 §5.4.1 (see §12.4) |
| DNS 0x20 case randomisation | about 1 extra bit per letter in QNAME | §16.3 |
| DNS Cookies | off-path attackers cannot know the server cookie | RFC 7873 |
| Fallback to TCP when a spoofing attempt is detected (e.g. many mismatched replies) | TCP needs a handshake | RFC 5452 §9.3 |
| **DNSSEC validation** | the only cryptographic fix: forged data does not validate | §15 |

Later research found ways around randomisation: inferring the source port through ICMP rate-limit side channels ("SAD DNS", 2020, CVE-2020-25705), and spoofing the second **IP fragment** of a large response, which carries no DNS header (Herzberg & Shulman, 2013). Defences include avoiding fragmentation altogether, by using an EDNS size of 1232 (§9.4) and setting DF, as well as cookies and DNSSEC.

### 16.2 ID and port randomisation in practice

- Use the OS **CSPRNG**: `getrandom()` on Linux, `arc4random()` on BSD/macOS, `BCryptGenRandom` on Windows. **Never** use `rand()`, `std::mt19937` seeded with the time, or a counter. The §17 code uses `getrandom()`:

  ```cpp
  inline uint16_t random16() {
      uint16_t v;
      if (getrandom(&v, sizeof v, 0) != sizeof v) throw std::runtime_error("getrandom failed");
      return v;
  }
  ```

- **Source port:** open a new UDP socket per query (or use a large pool), and `bind()` to port 0 so the kernel picks a random ephemeral port. Linux randomises them by default. `connect()` the socket to the server, so the kernel drops datagrams from any other address or port.
- Do not reuse IDs across outstanding queries, and never forward a client's ID upstream (§12.8).
- **Entropy budget** (RFC 5452 §7): with ID (16 bits), about 2¹⁵–2¹⁶ usable ports, and N authoritative server addresses to choose from, an attacker needs on the order of 2³² × N spoofed packets for a likely win. With 0x20, multiply by 2 for every letter in the QNAME.

### 16.3 DNS 0x20 case randomisation

Most authoritative servers echo the question **byte for byte**, case included. A resolver can randomise the case of each letter in the QNAME and require the response to match exactly. The idea comes from draft-vixie-dnsext-dns0x20 (2008). The name refers to 0x20, the bit that separates upper- from lower-case ASCII letters.

```cpp
// DNS 0x20 (draft-vixie-dnsext-dns0x20): randomise the case of every letter; a genuine
// reply echoes the question byte-for-byte, an off-path forger has to guess the pattern.
inline DnsName randomizeCase(const DnsName& n) {
    DnsName out;
    for (std::string label : n.labels()) {
        for (char& c : label) {
            char lower = asciiLower(c);
            if (lower >= 'a' && lower <= 'z') c = (random16() & 1) ? char(lower - 32) : lower;
        }
        out.appendLabel(std::move(label));
    }
    return out;
}
```

Responses are compared with `exactSameName()`, which is case-*sensitive* and is used only for this check. Some servers do not preserve case. Production resolvers detect that and turn 0x20 off for those servers. The §17 resolver simply treats such answers as invalid and tries the next server.

**Authoritative servers must preserve the question's case.** Echo it exactly as received (§3.3). The §17 server copies `query.questions` unchanged.

### 16.4 Amplification and reflection

The attacker sends small queries with the **victim's address forged as the source**. The servers send large responses to the victim. DNS is attractive for this because a 40-byte query for `ANY` or a DNSSEC-signed name can return 3–4 KB, an amplification of 50–100×.

| Mitigation | Who | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| **No open resolvers.** Recursive service only for your own clients. | resolver | The §17 server recurses only for `127.0.0.0/8`. |
| **Response Rate Limiting (RRL)** | authoritative | Limit identical responses per client prefix per second. When over the limit, drop the response or "slip" a truncated (TC=1) one, so real clients retry over TCP and a spoofed victim gets nothing big. |
| **DNS Cookies** (RFC 7873) | both | Clients with a valid server cookie are exempt from RRL; spoofed sources cannot have one. |
| Minimal responses; RFC 8482 for ANY | authoritative | smaller responses mean less amplification (§6.20, §11.9) |
| EDNS size 1232 | both | caps UDP responses, avoids fragments |
| Ingress filtering, BCP 38 (RFC 2827) | networks | prevents source spoofing at the origin |

A minimal RRL design:

```text
key    = (client address masked to /24 or /56, response "class": the answer RRset, or the
          NXDOMAIN/referral zone, or "error")
bucket = token bucket per key, e.g. 5 responses per second, burst 5
on response:
    if cookie valid or transport is TCP: send
    elif bucket has a token:             take token, send
    elif counter % 2 == 0:               send TC=1 empty response ("slip"), much smaller
    else:                                drop
```

### 16.5 Malformed packets

Every field in a received packet is attacker-controlled. Classes of bugs found in real DNS software include out-of-bounds reads from label lengths, infinite loops through compression pointers, integer overflow in RDLENGTH arithmetic, stack exhaustion from recursion, and assertion failures on unexpected record combinations. The "NAME:WRECK" (2021) and "DNSpooq" (2021) sets of vulnerabilities are well-documented examples.

Rules:

1. **One bounds-checked reader** for all parsing (§17.3 `WireReader`). No raw pointer arithmetic anywhere else.
2. Compute lengths in `size_t`, and compare as `n > remaining` rather than `pos + n > size`, which avoids overflow.
3. **No recursion driven by input data.** Parse names iteratively. Bound CNAME chains, referrals and nested lookups.
4. Fuzz your parser. The §17 parser was run on 300,000 randomly mutated messages, plus a 30-second coverage-guided libFuzzer run of about 414,000 inputs, both under ASan/UBSan (§18.5). Run libFuzzer or AFL++ for hours, not seconds.
5. Treat "impossible" combinations as errors, not assertions: two OPT records, OPT outside the additional section, TSIG not last, a QR=1 message on the server port.

### 16.6 Resource-exhaustion attacks

| Attack | Mechanism | Defence |
|---|---|---|
| Compression pointer chains / loops | pointer loops burn CPU or hang the parser | §5.6: strictly decreasing pointer targets, 255-octet limit |
| **Random subdomain** ("water torture") | queries for `<random>.victim.example` defeat caching and hammer the authoritative servers | RFC 8198 aggressive NSEC use; per-zone rate limits |
| **NXNSAttack** (2020, CVE-2020-8616 in BIND) | a referral with many glueless NS names makes the resolver resolve them all | limit the NS names resolved per referral, and the queries per client request |
| **KeyTrap** (2023, CVE-2023-50387) | DNSSEC zones with many keys sharing a key tag and many signatures cause enormous validation work | cap signature validations per response and per query |
| NSEC3 iterations (CVE-2023-50868) | expensive closest-encloser proofs | RFC 9276 limits (§15.6) |
| TCP connection exhaustion / slow reads | idle or slowly reading connections hold file descriptors | §8.5: connection caps, idle timeouts reset only on full messages, output caps |
| Large CNAME / DNAME chains | many hops per query | chain limits (§11.3: 16; §12.6: 12) |

The common lesson: **every loop and every per-query cost needs an explicit, low upper bound.**

### 16.7 Input validation checklist

**Message level**

- [ ] Length ≥ 12, else drop. QR=1 on a server socket → drop.
- [ ] Opcode checked before parsing the body (NOTIMP).
- [ ] QDCOUNT = 1 for QUERY (RFC 9619); other counts bounded by the remaining bytes.
- [ ] No bytes left after the last record (or a deliberate decision to ignore them).
- [ ] At most one OPT; OPT only in the additional section; OPT owner = root.
- [ ] TSIG, if present, is the last record.

**Names**

- [ ] Label length ≤ 63; type bits `01` and `10` rejected.
- [ ] Pointers strictly backwards (below the lowest offset visited), within bounds.
- [ ] Decoded length ≤ 255 octets.

**Records**

- [ ] RDLENGTH ≤ remaining bytes.
- [ ] Structured RDATA consumes exactly RDLENGTH (A = 4, AAAA = 16, …).
- [ ] TTL capped (RFC 8767 §4) and handled as unsigned.
- [ ] EDNS option lengths stay within RDLENGTH; cookie length 8 or 16–40; ECS address length matches the prefix.

**Resolver-side**

- [ ] Response matches the query in address, port, ID, QNAME (case-exact under 0x20), QTYPE and QCLASS.
- [ ] Bailiwick filtering on all sections.
- [ ] Trust ranking on cache insertion.
- [ ] Limits on referrals, CNAME hops, NS lookups, total time and DNSSEC validations.

**Operational**

- [ ] Drop privileges after binding port 53, or grant only `CAP_NET_BIND_SERVICE`.
- [ ] Escape names before logging them (§3.5); never print raw bytes from the network.
- [ ] Restrict zone transfers (ACL/TSIG) and recursion (ACL).

## 17. Building a DNS Server Step by Step

This section builds a complete, working server in ten stages: authoritative for zone files, and optionally an iterative resolver. Every listing below is the **complete, compiled and tested** source. Nothing is elided.

### 17.1 Layout, build, and how it fits `easydns`

```text
include/dns/wire.hpp        WireReader, WireWriter (compression), DnsName           stages 2, 5
include/dns/message.hpp     Header, Question, ResourceRecord, Edns, Message,
                            parseMessage, serializeMessage (truncation, OPT)        stages 2, 5, 7, 8
include/dns/zone.hpp        Zone, Node, RRset, master-file tokenizer and parser    stage 4
include/dns/authority.hpp   AuthoritativeServer: the RFC 1034 §4.3.2 lookup         stage 6
include/dns/resolver.hpp    Cache, UDP/TCP exchange, iterative Resolver             stage 9
src/server.cpp              sockets, poll loop, UDP + TCP, access control          stages 1, 7, 10
```

The include paths (`#include <dns/wire.hpp>`) match the existing `easydns` layout: the Makefile already passes `-Iinclude`, and `include/dns/` already exists. The files are header-only, apart from `server.cpp`, so you can drop them in next to `header.h`, `request.hpp` and `response.hpp`. The `Message` struct covers what `DnsRequest`/`DnsResponse` started on (header getters and setters, `encode`/`decode`); you can implement those classes on top of `parseMessage`/`serializeMessage`, or replace them.

Build and run, standalone:

```bash
clang++ -std=c++20 -O2 -Wall -Wextra -Iinclude src/server.cpp -o dnsd
# development build with sanitizers (recommended while working on the parser):
clang++ -std=c++20 -g -Wall -Wextra -fsanitize=address,undefined -Iinclude src/server.cpp -o dnsd

./dnsd -p 5353 -z example.com. example.com.zone          # authoritative only
./dnsd -p 5353 -z example.com. example.com.zone -r       # + recursion for 127.0.0.0/8
```

Port 5353 avoids needing root. Port 53 needs root or `CAP_NET_BIND_SERVICE` (§16.7). On systems that run mDNS, 5353 may already be taken; then pick another port such as 5300.

### 17.2 Stage 1 — a UDP socket that receives and echoes

**Goal:** prove you can receive datagrams and reply to the sender. Note `htons()`: ports and addresses in socket structures are in network byte order.

```cpp
// Stage 1: receive UDP datagrams on port 5353 and send each one back unchanged.
#include <arpa/inet.h>
#include <netinet/in.h>
#include <sys/socket.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>

int main() {
    int fd = socket(AF_INET, SOCK_DGRAM, 0);
    if (fd < 0) { perror("socket"); return 1; }

    sockaddr_in addr{};
    addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    addr.sin_port = htons(5353);                 // network byte order!
    addr.sin_addr.s_addr = htonl(INADDR_LOOPBACK);
    if (bind(fd, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&addr), sizeof addr) < 0) { perror("bind"); return 1; }

    uint8_t buf[65535];                          // the largest possible UDP payload
    for (;;) {
        sockaddr_in from{};
        socklen_t fromLen = sizeof from;
        ssize_t n = recvfrom(fd, buf, sizeof buf, 0, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&from), &fromLen);
        if (n < 0) { perror("recvfrom"); continue; }
        char ip[INET_ADDRSTRLEN];
        inet_ntop(AF_INET, &from.sin_addr, ip, sizeof ip);
        std::printf("%zd bytes from %s:%u\n", n, ip, ntohs(from.sin_port));
        std::fflush(stdout);
        sendto(fd, buf, size_t(n), 0, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&from), fromLen);
    }
}
```

**Test:**

```bash
clang++ -std=c++20 -Wall -Wextra stage1_echo.cpp -o stage1_echo && ./stage1_echo &
python3 -c "import socket; s=socket.socket(socket.AF_INET,socket.SOCK_DGRAM); s.sendto(b'hello dns',('127.0.0.1',5353)); print(s.recv(100))"
# → b'hello dns'   and the server prints "9 bytes from 127.0.0.1:<port>"
dig @127.0.0.1 -p 5353 example.com     # dig complains about the reply: it is the query, QR=0, echoed back
```

### 17.3 Stage 2 — parsing the header and question (the wire layer)

**Goal:** turn bytes into a `Message` and back, safely. This is the most security-critical code in the server. Everything reads through `WireReader`, which bounds-checks every access (§4.7). Names are decoded with loop-proof pointer following (§5.6), and RDATA names are decompressed on input and recompressed on output (§5.3).

`include/dns/wire.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <span>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

namespace dns {

struct ParseError : std::runtime_error {
    using std::runtime_error::runtime_error;
};

// Bounds-checked big-endian reader over one complete DNS message.
// Every read checks the remaining length first; a short buffer throws ParseError.
class WireReader {
public:
    explicit WireReader(std::span<const uint8_t> msg) : msg_(msg) {}

    uint8_t u8() {
        need(1);
        return msg_[pos_++];
    }
    uint16_t u16() {
        need(2);
        uint16_t v = uint16_t(msg_[pos_] << 8 | msg_[pos_ + 1]);
        pos_ += 2;
        return v;
    }
    uint32_t u32() {
        need(4);
        uint32_t v = uint32_t(msg_[pos_]) << 24 | uint32_t(msg_[pos_ + 1]) << 16 |
                     uint32_t(msg_[pos_ + 2]) << 8 | uint32_t(msg_[pos_ + 3]);
        pos_ += 4;
        return v;
    }
    std::span<const uint8_t> bytes(size_t n) {
        need(n);
        auto s = msg_.subspan(pos_, n);
        pos_ += n;
        return s;
    }

    size_t pos() const { return pos_; }
    size_t remaining() const { return msg_.size() - pos_; }
    void seek(size_t p) {
        if (p > msg_.size()) throw ParseError("seek past end of message");
        pos_ = p;
    }
    std::span<const uint8_t> message() const { return msg_; }

private:
    void need(size_t n) const {
        if (n > msg_.size() - pos_) throw ParseError("unexpected end of message");
    }

    std::span<const uint8_t> msg_;
    size_t pos_ = 0;
};

inline char asciiLower(char c) { return (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') ? char(c + ('a' - 'A')) : c; }

// A domain name as a list of labels, leftmost label first. The root name has no labels.
// Labels hold raw bytes (any octet is legal) with their original case preserved;
// comparisons are ASCII case-insensitive (RFC 4343).
class DnsName {
public:
    static constexpr size_t kMaxLabel = 63;   // RFC 1035 §2.3.4
    static constexpr size_t kMaxWire = 255;   // RFC 1035 §2.3.4, includes length octets and root

    DnsName() = default;  // the root name "."

    static DnsName fromText(std::string_view text, const DnsName& origin = {});
    static DnsName read(WireReader& r);  // wire format, follows compression pointers
    static DnsName fromWire(std::span<const uint8_t> uncompressed) {
        WireReader r(uncompressed);
        return read(r);
    }

    std::string toText() const;
    const std::vector<std::string>& labels() const { return labels_; }
    size_t labelCount() const { return labels_.size(); }
    bool isRoot() const { return labels_.empty(); }
    size_t wireLength() const {
        size_t n = 1;
        for (const auto& l : labels_) n += 1 + l.size();
        return n;
    }

    void appendLabel(std::string label) {
        if (label.empty() || label.size() > kMaxLabel) throw ParseError("label length out of range");
        if (wireLength() + 1 + label.size() > kMaxWire) throw ParseError("name longer than 255 octets");
        labels_.push_back(std::move(label));
    }
    DnsName parent() const {  // drop the leftmost label; the root is its own parent
        DnsName p;
        if (!labels_.empty()) p.labels_.assign(labels_.begin() + 1, labels_.end());
        return p;
    }
    DnsName suffix(size_t n) const {  // the rightmost n labels
        DnsName s;
        s.labels_.assign(labels_.end() - long(n), labels_.end());
        return s;
    }
    DnsName withPrefix(std::string label) const {
        DnsName n;
        n.appendLabel(std::move(label));
        for (const auto& l : labels_) n.appendLabel(l);
        return n;
    }
    bool isSubdomainOf(const DnsName& ancestor) const {  // true for equal names as well
        if (ancestor.labels_.size() > labels_.size()) return false;
        size_t off = labels_.size() - ancestor.labels_.size();
        for (size_t i = 0; i < ancestor.labels_.size(); ++i)
            if (!labelEqual(labels_[off + i], ancestor.labels_[i])) return false;
        return true;
    }
    // Lower-cased uncompressed wire form: a unique key for hash maps.
    std::string key() const {
        std::string k;
        for (const auto& l : labels_) {
            k.push_back(char(l.size()));
            for (char c : l) k.push_back(asciiLower(c));
        }
        k.push_back('\0');
        return k;
    }
    bool operator==(const DnsName& o) const {
        if (labels_.size() != o.labels_.size()) return false;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < labels_.size(); ++i)
            if (!labelEqual(labels_[i], o.labels_[i])) return false;
        return true;
    }

private:
    static bool labelEqual(const std::string& a, const std::string& b) {
        if (a.size() != b.size()) return false;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < a.size(); ++i)
            if (asciiLower(a[i]) != asciiLower(b[i])) return false;
        return true;
    }

    std::vector<std::string> labels_;
};

// Presentation format -> name. Handles "\." "\\" "\DDD" escapes, "@" and relative names.
inline DnsName DnsName::fromText(std::string_view text, const DnsName& origin) {
    if (text == "@") return origin;
    if (text == ".") return DnsName{};
    if (text.empty()) throw ParseError("empty name");

    DnsName n;
    std::string label;
    bool absolute = false;
    auto isDigit = [](char c) { return c >= '0' && c <= '9'; };
    for (size_t i = 0; i < text.size(); ++i) {
        char c = text[i];
        if (c == '\\') {
            if (i + 1 >= text.size()) throw ParseError("dangling backslash in name");
            if (isDigit(text[i + 1])) {
                if (i + 3 >= text.size() || !isDigit(text[i + 2]) || !isDigit(text[i + 3]))
                    throw ParseError("malformed \\DDD escape");
                int v = (text[i + 1] - '0') * 100 + (text[i + 2] - '0') * 10 + (text[i + 3] - '0');
                if (v > 255) throw ParseError("\\DDD escape above 255");
                label.push_back(char(v));
                i += 3;
            } else {
                label.push_back(text[++i]);
            }
        } else if (c == '.') {
            if (label.empty()) throw ParseError("empty label in name");
            n.appendLabel(std::move(label));
            label.clear();
            if (i + 1 == text.size()) absolute = true;
        } else {
            label.push_back(c);
        }
    }
    if (!label.empty()) n.appendLabel(std::move(label));
    if (!absolute)
        for (const auto& l : origin.labels_) n.appendLabel(l);
    return n;
}

inline std::string DnsName::toText() const {
    if (labels_.empty()) return ".";
    static const std::string_view special = ".\\\"()@;$";
    std::string out;
    for (const auto& l : labels_) {
        for (char ch : l) {
            auto c = uint8_t(ch);
            if (c <= 0x20 || c >= 0x7f) {
                char buf[5];
                std::snprintf(buf, sizeof buf, "\\%03u", unsigned(c));
                out += buf;
            } else {
                if (special.find(ch) != std::string_view::npos) out.push_back('\\');
                out.push_back(ch);
            }
        }
        out.push_back('.');
    }
    return out;
}

// Reads a possibly-compressed name starting at r.pos() (RFC 1035 §4.1.4).
// On return r is positioned just after the name as it appears at the original position
// (after the first pointer, if any). Defences:
//   - every byte access is bounds-checked
//   - a pointer must point strictly before the lowest offset visited so far, so the
//     walk can never revisit a byte and always terminates (rejects loops and forward pointers)
//   - the decoded name may not exceed 255 octets
//   - label types 01 and 10 (RFC 6891 §5 deprecated extended labels) are rejected
inline DnsName DnsName::read(WireReader& r) {
    auto msg = r.message();
    size_t pos = r.pos();
    size_t lowest = pos;   // lowest offset this name has touched
    size_t resume = 0;     // where the reader continues after the name
    bool jumped = false;
    DnsName name;

    for (;;) {
        if (pos >= msg.size()) throw ParseError("name runs past end of message");
        uint8_t len = msg[pos];
        switch (len & 0xC0) {
        case 0x00:
            if (len == 0) {
                r.seek(jumped ? resume : pos + 1);
                return name;
            }
            if (pos + 1 + len > msg.size()) throw ParseError("label runs past end of message");
            name.appendLabel(std::string(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&msg[pos + 1]), len));
            pos += 1 + len;
            break;
        case 0xC0: {
            if (pos + 1 >= msg.size()) throw ParseError("truncated compression pointer");
            size_t target = size_t(len & 0x3F) << 8 | msg[pos + 1];
            if (!jumped) {
                resume = pos + 2;
                jumped = true;
            }
            if (target >= lowest) throw ParseError("compression pointer does not point backwards");
            lowest = target;
            pos = target;
            break;
        }
        default:
            throw ParseError("unsupported label type");
        }
    }
}

// Big-endian writer with name compression (RFC 1035 §4.1.4).
class WireWriter {
public:
    void u8(uint8_t v) { buf_.push_back(v); }
    void u16(uint16_t v) {
        buf_.push_back(uint8_t(v >> 8));
        buf_.push_back(uint8_t(v));
    }
    void u32(uint32_t v) {
        u16(uint16_t(v >> 16));
        u16(uint16_t(v));
    }
    void bytes(std::span<const uint8_t> b) { buf_.insert(buf_.end(), b.begin(), b.end()); }
    void patchU16(size_t at, uint16_t v) {
        buf_[at] = uint8_t(v >> 8);
        buf_[at + 1] = uint8_t(v);
    }

    // Writes a name. With compress=true the longest already-written suffix is replaced
    // by a pointer. Every suffix written in full becomes a pointer target for later names.
    void name(const DnsName& n, bool compress = true) {
        const auto& labels = n.labels();
        for (size_t i = 0; i < labels.size(); ++i) {
            std::string key = n.suffix(labels.size() - i).key();
            if (compress) {
                if (auto it = offsets_.find(key); it != offsets_.end()) {
                    u16(uint16_t(0xC000 | it->second));
                    return;
                }
            }
            if (buf_.size() <= 0x3FFF) offsets_.emplace(std::move(key), uint16_t(buf_.size()));
            u8(uint8_t(labels[i].size()));
            bytes({reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(labels[i].data()), labels[i].size()});
        }
        u8(0);
    }

    size_t size() const { return buf_.size(); }
    // Rolls the buffer back to len bytes and forgets compression targets beyond it,
    // so later names cannot point into discarded bytes.
    void truncate(size_t len) {
        buf_.resize(len);
        std::erase_if(offsets_, [len](const auto& kv) { return kv.second >= len; });
    }
    std::vector<uint8_t>& data() { return buf_; }

private:
    std::vector<uint8_t> buf_;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, uint16_t> offsets_;  // lower-cased suffix -> offset
};

}  // namespace dns
```

`include/dns/message.hpp`, which already contains the code that stages 5, 7 and 8 use (compression, truncation, OPT):

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <dns/wire.hpp>

#include <optional>

namespace dns {

namespace rrtype {
constexpr uint16_t A = 1, NS = 2, CNAME = 5, SOA = 6, PTR = 12, HINFO = 13, MX = 15, TXT = 16,
                   AAAA = 28, SRV = 33, NAPTR = 35, DNAME = 39, OPT = 41, DS = 43, RRSIG = 46,
                   NSEC = 47, DNSKEY = 48, NSEC3 = 50, NSEC3PARAM = 51, SVCB = 64, HTTPS = 65,
                   IXFR = 251, AXFR = 252, ANY = 255, CAA = 257;
}
namespace rrclass {
constexpr uint16_t IN = 1, CH = 3, HS = 4, NONE = 254, ANY = 255;
}
namespace rcode {
constexpr uint16_t NOERROR = 0, FORMERR = 1, SERVFAIL = 2, NXDOMAIN = 3, NOTIMP = 4, REFUSED = 5,
                   YXDOMAIN = 6, BADVERS = 16;
}
namespace opcode {
constexpr uint8_t QUERY = 0, NOTIFY = 4, UPDATE = 5;
}

// The 12-byte header (RFC 1035 §4.1.1, AD/CD from RFC 4035 §3.2).
struct Header {
    uint16_t id = 0;
    bool qr = false;
    uint8_t opcode = 0;  // 4 bits
    bool aa = false, tc = false, rd = false, ra = false, z = false, ad = false, cd = false;
    uint8_t rcode = 0;  // low 4 bits of the RCODE; the high 8 bits live in OPT
    uint16_t qdcount = 0, ancount = 0, nscount = 0, arcount = 0;

    uint16_t flags() const {
        return uint16_t((qr ? 0x8000 : 0) | (opcode & 0xF) << 11 | (aa ? 0x0400 : 0) |
                        (tc ? 0x0200 : 0) | (rd ? 0x0100 : 0) | (ra ? 0x0080 : 0) |
                        (z ? 0x0040 : 0) | (ad ? 0x0020 : 0) | (cd ? 0x0010 : 0) | (rcode & 0xF));
    }
    void setFlags(uint16_t f) {
        qr = f & 0x8000;
        opcode = (f >> 11) & 0xF;
        aa = f & 0x0400;
        tc = f & 0x0200;
        rd = f & 0x0100;
        ra = f & 0x0080;
        z = f & 0x0040;
        ad = f & 0x0020;
        cd = f & 0x0010;
        rcode = f & 0xF;
    }
};

struct Question {
    DnsName qname;
    uint16_t qtype = 0;
    uint16_t qclass = rrclass::IN;
};

struct ResourceRecord {
    DnsName name;
    uint16_t type = 0;
    uint16_t rclass = rrclass::IN;
    uint32_t ttl = 0;
    std::vector<uint8_t> rdata;  // always stored uncompressed
};

struct EdnsOption {
    uint16_t code = 0;
    std::vector<uint8_t> data;
};

// The OPT pseudo-RR, decoded (RFC 6891 §6.1).
struct Edns {
    uint16_t udpSize = 1232;
    uint8_t extendedRcode = 0;  // upper 8 bits of the 12-bit RCODE
    uint8_t version = 0;
    bool dnssecOk = false;  // DO bit
    std::vector<EdnsOption> options;
};

struct Message {
    Header header;
    std::vector<Question> questions;
    std::vector<ResourceRecord> answers, authority, additional;  // additional excludes OPT
    std::optional<Edns> edns;
    size_t requiredAdditional = 0;  // the first N additional records are mandatory glue (RFC 9471 §3.1)

    uint16_t rcode() const { return uint16_t(header.rcode | (edns ? edns->extendedRcode << 4 : 0)); }
    void setRcode(uint16_t rc) {
        header.rcode = rc & 0xF;
        if (edns) edns->extendedRcode = uint8_t(rc >> 4);
    }
};

// RDATA field layout for types that embed domain names: 'N' name, '2' 16-bit, '4' 32-bit.
// Bytes after the last field are opaque. Types not listed are copied verbatim.
inline const char* rdataLayout(uint16_t type) {
    switch (type) {
    case rrtype::NS: case rrtype::CNAME: case rrtype::PTR: case rrtype::DNAME: return "N";
    case rrtype::MX: return "2N";
    case rrtype::SOA: return "NN44444";
    case rrtype::SRV: return "222N";
    default: return nullptr;
    }
}

// Only the RFC 1035 types may be compressed on output (RFC 3597 §4).
inline bool mayCompressRdata(uint16_t type) {
    return type == rrtype::NS || type == rrtype::CNAME || type == rrtype::PTR ||
           type == rrtype::MX || type == rrtype::SOA;
}

// Copies the RDATA from the wire, expanding any compressed names inside it.
inline std::vector<uint8_t> readRdata(WireReader& r, uint16_t type, uint16_t rdlength) {
    if (rdlength > r.remaining()) throw ParseError("RDLENGTH exceeds message");
    size_t end = r.pos() + rdlength;
    const char* layout = rdataLayout(type);
    if (!layout) {
        auto b = r.bytes(rdlength);
        return {b.begin(), b.end()};
    }
    WireWriter out;
    for (const char* f = layout; *f; ++f) {
        switch (*f) {
        case 'N': out.name(DnsName::read(r), false); break;
        case '2': out.u16(r.u16()); break;
        case '4': out.u32(r.u32()); break;
        }
        if (r.pos() > end) throw ParseError("RDATA overruns RDLENGTH");
    }
    if (r.pos() != end) throw ParseError("RDATA shorter than RDLENGTH");
    return std::move(out.data());
}

inline void writeRdata(WireWriter& w, uint16_t type, const std::vector<uint8_t>& rdata) {
    const char* layout = mayCompressRdata(type) ? rdataLayout(type) : nullptr;
    if (!layout) {
        w.bytes(rdata);
        return;
    }
    WireReader r(rdata);
    for (const char* f = layout; *f; ++f) {
        switch (*f) {
        case 'N': w.name(DnsName::read(r)); break;
        case '2': w.u16(r.u16()); break;
        case '4': w.u32(r.u32()); break;
        }
    }
    w.bytes(r.bytes(r.remaining()));
}

inline void writeRecord(WireWriter& w, const ResourceRecord& rr) {
    w.name(rr.name);
    w.u16(rr.type);
    w.u16(rr.rclass);
    w.u32(rr.ttl);
    size_t lenAt = w.size();
    w.u16(0);  // RDLENGTH placeholder
    writeRdata(w, rr.type, rr.rdata);
    w.patchU16(lenAt, uint16_t(w.size() - lenAt - 2));
}

inline Edns parseOpt(uint16_t rclass, uint32_t ttl, std::span<const uint8_t> rdata) {
    Edns e;
    e.udpSize = rclass;
    e.extendedRcode = uint8_t(ttl >> 24);
    e.version = uint8_t(ttl >> 16);
    e.dnssecOk = ttl & 0x8000;
    WireReader r(rdata);
    while (r.remaining() > 0) {
        EdnsOption o;
        o.code = r.u16();
        uint16_t len = r.u16();
        auto d = r.bytes(len);
        o.data.assign(d.begin(), d.end());
        e.options.push_back(std::move(o));
    }
    return e;
}

inline void writeOpt(WireWriter& w, const Edns& e) {
    w.u8(0);  // owner: root
    w.u16(rrtype::OPT);
    w.u16(e.udpSize);
    w.u32(uint32_t(e.extendedRcode) << 24 | uint32_t(e.version) << 16 | (e.dnssecOk ? 0x8000u : 0u));
    size_t lenAt = w.size();
    w.u16(0);
    for (const auto& o : e.options) {
        w.u16(o.code);
        w.u16(uint16_t(o.data.size()));
        w.bytes(o.data);
    }
    w.patchU16(lenAt, uint16_t(w.size() - lenAt - 2));
}

inline Message parseMessage(std::span<const uint8_t> wire) {
    WireReader r(wire);
    Message m;
    m.header.id = r.u16();
    m.header.setFlags(r.u16());
    uint16_t qd = r.u16(), an = r.u16(), ns = r.u16(), ar = r.u16();
    m.header.qdcount = qd;
    m.header.ancount = an;
    m.header.nscount = ns;
    m.header.arcount = ar;

    for (uint16_t i = 0; i < qd; ++i) {
        Question q;
        q.qname = DnsName::read(r);
        q.qtype = r.u16();
        q.qclass = r.u16();
        m.questions.push_back(std::move(q));
    }
    auto readSection = [&](uint16_t count, std::vector<ResourceRecord>& out, bool isAdditional) {
        for (uint16_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
            ResourceRecord rr;
            rr.name = DnsName::read(r);
            rr.type = r.u16();
            rr.rclass = r.u16();
            rr.ttl = r.u32();
            uint16_t rdlength = r.u16();
            if (rr.type == rrtype::OPT) {  // RFC 6891 §6.1.1: one OPT, owner root, additional only
                if (!isAdditional || m.edns || !rr.name.isRoot()) throw ParseError("invalid OPT record");
                m.edns = parseOpt(rr.rclass, rr.ttl, r.bytes(rdlength));
                continue;
            }
            rr.rdata = readRdata(r, rr.type, rdlength);
            out.push_back(std::move(rr));
        }
    };
    readSection(an, m.answers, false);
    readSection(ns, m.authority, false);
    readSection(ar, m.additional, true);
    if (r.remaining() != 0) throw ParseError("trailing bytes after last record");
    return m;
}

// Serialises m into at most maxSize bytes. If the answer or authority section, or required
// glue, does not fit, the response is cut back to header + question with TC=1 (RFC 2181 §9,
// RFC 9471 §3.1). Other additional records that do not fit are dropped without TC.
// OPT is always included.
inline std::vector<uint8_t> serializeMessage(const Message& m, size_t maxSize = 65535) {
    WireWriter w;
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) w.u8(0);  // header, patched at the end
    for (const auto& q : m.questions) {
        w.name(q.qname);
        w.u16(q.qtype);
        w.u16(q.qclass);
    }
    size_t afterQuestion = w.size();

    size_t optSize = 0;
    if (m.edns) {
        optSize = 11;
        for (const auto& o : m.edns->options) optSize += 4 + o.data.size();
    }
    size_t limit = maxSize > optSize ? maxSize - optSize : 0;

    Header h = m.header;
    h.qdcount = uint16_t(m.questions.size());
    h.ancount = h.nscount = h.arcount = 0;

    // Writes records until one does not fit; returns how many were written.
    auto writeSection = [&](const std::vector<ResourceRecord>& rrs, uint16_t& count) {
        for (const auto& rr : rrs) {
            size_t mark = w.size();
            writeRecord(w, rr);
            if (w.size() > limit) {
                w.truncate(mark);
                break;
            }
            ++count;
        }
        return size_t(count);
    };
    if (writeSection(m.answers, h.ancount) < m.answers.size() ||
        writeSection(m.authority, h.nscount) < m.authority.size() ||
        writeSection(m.additional, h.arcount) < m.requiredAdditional) {
        w.truncate(afterQuestion);
        h.ancount = h.nscount = h.arcount = 0;
        h.tc = true;
    }
    if (m.edns) {
        writeOpt(w, *m.edns);
        ++h.arcount;
    }

    w.patchU16(0, h.id);
    w.patchU16(2, h.flags());
    w.patchU16(4, h.qdcount);
    w.patchU16(6, h.ancount);
    w.patchU16(8, h.nscount);
    w.patchU16(10, h.arcount);
    return std::move(w.data());
}

}  // namespace dns
```

**Data structures in this stage:**

| Type | Holds |
|---|---|
| `DnsName` | labels as raw bytes, case preserved; `key()` = lower-cased wire form for maps |
| `Header` | the 12-byte header as booleans and small integers; `flags()`/`setFlags()` pack the second word |
| `ResourceRecord` | owner, type, class, TTL, **uncompressed** RDATA bytes |
| `Edns` | decoded OPT: payload size, extended RCODE, version, DO, options |
| `Message` | header, question, three record sections, optional EDNS |

**Test:** the unit test in §18.4 parses the §4.5 query and checks every field. It re-serialises every hex example in this guide and compares the bytes, and it feeds the hostile inputs from §5.6.

### 17.4 Stage 3 — answering a hard-coded A record

**Goal:** a server that `dig` accepts. Copy the ID, set QR, copy RD and the question, add one answer.

```cpp
// Stage 3: answer every "A" question with 192.0.2.1, everything else with NOTIMP/NODATA.
#include <dns/message.hpp>

#include <netinet/in.h>
#include <sys/socket.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#include <cstdio>

using namespace dns;

int main() {
    int fd = socket(AF_INET, SOCK_DGRAM, 0);
    sockaddr_in addr{};
    addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    addr.sin_port = htons(5353);
    addr.sin_addr.s_addr = htonl(INADDR_LOOPBACK);
    if (fd < 0 || bind(fd, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&addr), sizeof addr) < 0) { perror("bind"); return 1; }

    uint8_t buf[65535];
    for (;;) {
        sockaddr_in from{};
        socklen_t fromLen = sizeof from;
        ssize_t n = recvfrom(fd, buf, sizeof buf, 0, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&from), &fromLen);
        if (n < 12 || (buf[2] & 0x80)) continue;  // too short, or a response: ignore

        Message q;
        try {
            q = parseMessage({buf, size_t(n)});
        } catch (const ParseError& e) {
            std::fprintf(stderr, "bad query: %s\n", e.what());
            continue;
        }

        Message r;
        r.header.id = q.header.id;   // copy ID ...
        r.header.qr = true;          // ... mark as response ...
        r.header.rd = q.header.rd;   // ... and copy RD
        r.header.opcode = q.header.opcode;
        r.questions = q.questions;   // echo the question exactly
        if (q.header.opcode != opcode::QUERY || q.questions.size() != 1) {
            r.header.rcode = rcode::NOTIMP;
        } else {
            r.header.aa = true;
            const Question& question = q.questions[0];
            if (question.qtype == rrtype::A && question.qclass == rrclass::IN)
                r.answers.push_back({question.qname, rrtype::A, rrclass::IN, 300, {192, 0, 2, 1}});
            // any other type: NOERROR with an empty answer (NODATA)
        }
        auto out = serializeMessage(r, 512);
        sendto(fd, out.data(), out.size(), 0, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&from), fromLen);
    }
}
```

**Test:**

```bash
clang++ -std=c++20 -Wall -Wextra -Iinclude stage3_hardcoded.cpp -o stage3 && ./stage3 &
dig @127.0.0.1 -p 5353 anything.test A +norec
# ;; flags: qr aa; QUERY: 1, ANSWER: 1 …   anything.test. 300 IN A 192.0.2.1
dig @127.0.0.1 -p 5353 anything.test MX +norec      # NOERROR, ANSWER: 0 (NODATA)
```

This stage still has two flaws, which later stages fix. It sends no OPT back when the query had one, so `dig` warns. And it claims to be authoritative for every name.

### 17.5 Stage 4 — loading records from a zone file

**Goal:** parse the master-file format (§10) into an in-memory zone: a hash map from name to node, with empty non-terminals created automatically. Validation follows §10.7.

`include/dns/zone.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <dns/message.hpp>

#include <arpa/inet.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <map>

namespace dns {

// All records sharing owner, class and type (RFC 2181 §5).
struct RRset {
    DnsName name;
    uint16_t type = 0;
    uint16_t rclass = rrclass::IN;
    uint32_t ttl = 0;
    std::vector<std::vector<uint8_t>> rdatas;
};

struct Node {
    DnsName name;
    std::map<uint16_t, RRset> rrsets;  // empty map = empty non-terminal

    const RRset* find(uint16_t type) const {
        auto it = rrsets.find(type);
        return it == rrsets.end() ? nullptr : &it->second;
    }
};

class Zone {
public:
    explicit Zone(DnsName origin) : origin_(std::move(origin)) { nodes_[origin_.key()].name = origin_; }

    const DnsName& origin() const { return origin_; }

    const Node* find(const DnsName& n) const {
        auto it = nodes_.find(n.key());
        return it == nodes_.end() ? nullptr : &it->second;
    }

    void add(const ResourceRecord& rr) {
        if (!rr.name.isSubdomainOf(origin_)) throw ParseError("record " + rr.name.toText() + " is outside the zone");
        // Make every ancestor between the owner and the apex exist, so empty non-terminals
        // answer NODATA instead of NXDOMAIN (RFC 8020).
        for (DnsName n = rr.name.parent(); n.labelCount() > origin_.labelCount(); n = n.parent())
            nodes_.try_emplace(n.key()).first->second.name = n;

        Node& node = nodes_[rr.name.key()];
        node.name = rr.name;
        bool hasCname = node.find(rrtype::CNAME) != nullptr;
        bool hasOther = std::any_of(node.rrsets.begin(), node.rrsets.end(), [](const auto& kv) {
            return kv.first != rrtype::CNAME && kv.first != rrtype::RRSIG && kv.first != rrtype::NSEC;
        });
        bool dnssecType = rr.type == rrtype::RRSIG || rr.type == rrtype::NSEC;
        // RFC 1034 §3.6.2 / RFC 2181 §10.1: a CNAME owner has no other data (except DNSSEC).
        if ((rr.type == rrtype::CNAME && hasOther) || (rr.type != rrtype::CNAME && !dnssecType && hasCname))
            throw ParseError("CNAME and other data at " + rr.name.toText());

        RRset& set = node.rrsets[rr.type];
        if (set.rdatas.empty()) {
            set.name = rr.name;
            set.type = rr.type;
            set.rclass = rr.rclass;
            set.ttl = rr.ttl;
        } else {
            if (rr.type == rrtype::CNAME) throw ParseError("multiple CNAMEs at " + rr.name.toText());
            set.ttl = std::min(set.ttl, rr.ttl);  // RFC 2181 §5.2: all TTLs in an RRset must match
        }
        if (std::find(set.rdatas.begin(), set.rdatas.end(), rr.rdata) == set.rdatas.end())
            set.rdatas.push_back(rr.rdata);  // RFC 2181 §5: drop duplicate RRs
    }

    // A loadable zone has exactly one SOA and at least one NS at the apex (RFC 1035 §5.2).
    void validate() const {
        const Node* apex = find(origin_);
        const RRset* soa = apex->find(rrtype::SOA);
        if (!soa || soa->rdatas.size() != 1) throw ParseError("zone needs exactly one SOA at the apex");
        if (!apex->find(rrtype::NS)) throw ParseError("zone needs NS records at the apex");
    }

private:
    DnsName origin_;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, Node> nodes_;
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- master file parser

struct ZoneToken {
    std::string text;
    bool quoted = false;
};

struct ZoneLine {
    std::vector<ZoneToken> tokens;
    bool blankOwner = false;  // line started with whitespace: reuse previous owner
    size_t lineNo = 0;
};

// Splits a master file into logical lines (RFC 1035 §5.1): handles ';' comments,
// '(' ')' continuation, "quoted strings" and backslash escapes (kept for later decoding).
inline std::vector<ZoneLine> tokenizeZone(std::string_view s) {
    std::vector<ZoneLine> lines;
    ZoneLine cur;
    int parens = 0;
    size_t lineNo = 1;
    bool atLineStart = true;
    auto flush = [&] {
        if (!cur.tokens.empty()) lines.push_back(std::move(cur));
        cur = {};
    };
    auto isDelim = [](char c) {
        return c == ' ' || c == '\t' || c == '\r' || c == '\n' || c == ';' || c == '(' || c == ')' || c == '"';
    };

    size_t i = 0;
    while (i < s.size()) {
        char c = s[i];
        if (c == '\n') {
            ++lineNo;
            ++i;
            if (parens == 0) {
                flush();
                atLineStart = true;
            }
            continue;
        }
        if (c == ' ' || c == '\t' || c == '\r') {
            if (atLineStart) cur.blankOwner = true;
            atLineStart = false;
            ++i;
            continue;
        }
        atLineStart = false;
        if (c == ';') {
            while (i < s.size() && s[i] != '\n') ++i;
            continue;
        }
        if (c == '(') { ++parens; ++i; continue; }
        if (c == ')') {
            if (parens == 0) throw ParseError("unbalanced ')' on line " + std::to_string(lineNo));
            --parens;
            ++i;
            continue;
        }
        if (cur.tokens.empty()) cur.lineNo = lineNo;
        ZoneToken t;
        if (c == '"') {
            t.quoted = true;
            ++i;
            while (i < s.size() && s[i] != '"') {
                if (s[i] == '\\' && i + 1 < s.size()) t.text += s[i++];
                if (s[i] == '\n') ++lineNo;
                t.text += s[i++];
            }
            if (i >= s.size()) throw ParseError("unterminated string on line " + std::to_string(lineNo));
            ++i;  // closing quote
        } else {
            while (i < s.size() && !isDelim(s[i])) {
                if (s[i] == '\\' && i + 1 < s.size()) t.text += s[i++];
                t.text += s[i++];
            }
        }
        cur.tokens.push_back(std::move(t));
    }
    if (parens != 0) throw ParseError("unbalanced '(' at end of file");
    flush();
    return lines;
}

// "3600", "1h", "1h30m", "2w" -> seconds (unit suffixes are a BIND extension).
inline uint32_t parseTtl(std::string_view s) {
    if (s.empty()) throw ParseError("empty TTL");
    uint64_t total = 0, cur = 0;
    bool digits = false;
    for (char c : s) {
        if (c >= '0' && c <= '9') {
            cur = cur * 10 + uint64_t(c - '0');
            digits = true;
            if (cur > 0xFFFFFFFFull) throw ParseError("TTL overflow");
            continue;
        }
        uint64_t mult;
        switch (asciiLower(c)) {
        case 's': mult = 1; break;
        case 'm': mult = 60; break;
        case 'h': mult = 3600; break;
        case 'd': mult = 86400; break;
        case 'w': mult = 604800; break;
        default: throw ParseError("bad TTL: " + std::string(s));
        }
        if (!digits) throw ParseError("bad TTL: " + std::string(s));
        total += cur * mult;
        cur = 0;
        digits = false;
    }
    total += cur;
    if (total > 0x7FFFFFFF) throw ParseError("TTL above 2^31-1 (RFC 2181 §8)");
    return uint32_t(total);
}

inline uint32_t parseU32(std::string_view s, uint64_t max = 0xFFFFFFFF) {
    if (s.empty() || s.size() > 10) throw ParseError("bad number: " + std::string(s));
    uint64_t v = 0;
    for (char c : s) {
        if (c < '0' || c > '9') throw ParseError("bad number: " + std::string(s));
        v = v * 10 + uint64_t(c - '0');
    }
    if (v > max) throw ParseError("number out of range: " + std::string(s));
    return uint32_t(v);
}

// <character-string>: one length octet + up to 255 bytes (RFC 1035 §3.3).
inline void appendCharString(std::vector<uint8_t>& out, std::string_view text) {
    std::string raw;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < text.size(); ++i) {
        if (text[i] == '\\' && i + 1 < text.size()) {
            if (text[i + 1] >= '0' && text[i + 1] <= '9') {
                if (i + 3 >= text.size()) throw ParseError("malformed \\DDD escape");
                raw.push_back(char(parseU32(text.substr(i + 1, 3), 255)));
                i += 3;
            } else {
                raw.push_back(text[++i]);
            }
        } else {
            raw.push_back(text[i]);
        }
    }
    if (raw.size() > 255) throw ParseError("character-string longer than 255 bytes");
    out.push_back(uint8_t(raw.size()));
    out.insert(out.end(), raw.begin(), raw.end());
}

inline const std::vector<std::pair<std::string_view, uint16_t>>& typeTable() {
    static const std::vector<std::pair<std::string_view, uint16_t>> t = {
        {"A", rrtype::A},       {"NS", rrtype::NS},       {"CNAME", rrtype::CNAME}, {"SOA", rrtype::SOA},
        {"PTR", rrtype::PTR},   {"HINFO", rrtype::HINFO}, {"MX", rrtype::MX},       {"TXT", rrtype::TXT},
        {"AAAA", rrtype::AAAA}, {"SRV", rrtype::SRV},     {"DNAME", rrtype::DNAME}, {"CAA", rrtype::CAA},
        {"DS", rrtype::DS},     {"ANY", rrtype::ANY}};
    return t;
}

inline std::string upper(std::string_view s) {
    std::string u(s);
    for (char& c : u) c = (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') ? char(c - 32) : c;
    return u;
}

// "A" -> 1, "TYPE65280" -> 65280 (RFC 3597 §5). Returns nullopt for unknown mnemonics.
inline std::optional<uint16_t> typeFromText(std::string_view s) {
    std::string u = upper(s);
    for (const auto& [name, code] : typeTable())
        if (name == u) return code;
    if (u.starts_with("TYPE") && u.size() > 4) return uint16_t(parseU32(std::string_view(u).substr(4), 65535));
    return std::nullopt;
}

inline std::string typeToText(uint16_t type) {
    for (const auto& [name, code] : typeTable())
        if (code == type) return std::string(name);
    return "TYPE" + std::to_string(type);
}

// Converts the RDATA tokens of one record to uncompressed wire form.
inline std::vector<uint8_t> parseRdataText(uint16_t type, const std::vector<ZoneToken>& f, const DnsName& origin) {
    WireWriter w;
    auto needFields = [&](size_t n) {
        if (f.size() != n) throw ParseError(typeToText(type) + " needs " + std::to_string(n) + " RDATA fields");
    };
    auto name = [&](const ZoneToken& t) { w.name(DnsName::fromText(t.text, origin), false); };

    // RFC 3597 §5 generic form: \# <length> <hex...>
    if (!f.empty() && f[0].text == "\\#") {
        if (f.size() < 2) throw ParseError("\\# needs a length");
        uint32_t len = parseU32(f[1].text, 65535);
        std::string hex;
        for (size_t i = 2; i < f.size(); ++i) hex += f[i].text;
        if (hex.size() != size_t(len) * 2) throw ParseError("\\# length does not match hex data");
        for (size_t i = 0; i < hex.size(); i += 2) w.u8(uint8_t(std::stoul(hex.substr(i, 2), nullptr, 16)));
        return std::move(w.data());
    }

    switch (type) {
    case rrtype::A: {
        needFields(1);
        uint8_t b[4];
        if (inet_pton(AF_INET, f[0].text.c_str(), b) != 1) throw ParseError("bad IPv4 address " + f[0].text);
        w.bytes(b);
        break;
    }
    case rrtype::AAAA: {
        needFields(1);
        uint8_t b[16];
        if (inet_pton(AF_INET6, f[0].text.c_str(), b) != 1) throw ParseError("bad IPv6 address " + f[0].text);
        w.bytes(b);
        break;
    }
    case rrtype::NS: case rrtype::CNAME: case rrtype::PTR: case rrtype::DNAME:
        needFields(1);
        name(f[0]);
        break;
    case rrtype::MX:
        needFields(2);
        w.u16(uint16_t(parseU32(f[0].text, 65535)));
        name(f[1]);
        break;
    case rrtype::SRV:
        needFields(4);
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) w.u16(uint16_t(parseU32(f[size_t(i)].text, 65535)));
        name(f[3]);
        break;
    case rrtype::SOA:
        needFields(7);
        name(f[0]);  // MNAME
        name(f[1]);  // RNAME
        w.u32(parseU32(f[2].text));  // SERIAL
        for (int i = 3; i < 7; ++i) w.u32(parseTtl(f[size_t(i)].text));  // REFRESH RETRY EXPIRE MINIMUM
        break;
    case rrtype::TXT: case rrtype::HINFO: {
        if (type == rrtype::HINFO) needFields(2);
        if (f.empty()) throw ParseError("TXT needs at least one string");
        std::vector<uint8_t> out;
        for (const auto& t : f) appendCharString(out, t.text);
        w.bytes(out);
        break;
    }
    case rrtype::CAA: {  // RFC 8659 §4.1
        needFields(3);
        w.u8(uint8_t(parseU32(f[0].text, 255)));
        if (f[1].text.empty() || f[1].text.size() > 255) throw ParseError("bad CAA tag");
        w.u8(uint8_t(f[1].text.size()));
        w.bytes({reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(f[1].text.data()), f[1].text.size()});
        std::vector<uint8_t> value;
        appendCharString(value, f[2].text);
        w.bytes(std::span(value).subspan(1));  // the value has no length octet
        break;
    }
    default:
        throw ParseError("no text parser for " + typeToText(type) + "; use the \\# generic form");
    }
    return std::move(w.data());
}

// Parses a master file (RFC 1035 §5) into a Zone rooted at origin.
inline Zone parseZone(std::string_view text, DnsName origin, uint32_t defaultTtl = 3600) {
    Zone zone(origin);
    DnsName current = origin;  // $ORIGIN
    std::optional<DnsName> lastOwner;
    std::optional<uint32_t> ttlDirective, lastTtl;

    for (const ZoneLine& line : tokenizeZone(text)) {
        const auto& tok = line.tokens;
        auto fail = [&](const std::string& why) -> ParseError {
            return ParseError("line " + std::to_string(line.lineNo) + ": " + why);
        };
        try {
            if (!tok[0].quoted && tok[0].text.starts_with('$')) {
                std::string dir = upper(tok[0].text);
                if (dir == "$ORIGIN" && tok.size() == 2) current = DnsName::fromText(tok[1].text, current);
                else if (dir == "$TTL" && tok.size() == 2) ttlDirective = parseTtl(tok[1].text);
                else throw fail("unsupported or malformed directive " + tok[0].text);
                continue;
            }

            size_t i = 0;
            ResourceRecord rr;
            if (line.blankOwner) {
                if (!lastOwner) throw fail("first record has no owner");
                rr.name = *lastOwner;
            } else {
                rr.name = DnsName::fromText(tok[i++].text, current);
            }
            // [<TTL>] [<class>] or [<class>] [<TTL>], then <type>
            std::optional<uint32_t> ttl;
            for (int k = 0; k < 2 && i < tok.size(); ++k) {
                std::string u = upper(tok[i].text);
                if (u == "IN") { rr.rclass = rrclass::IN; ++i; }
                else if (u == "CH") { rr.rclass = rrclass::CH; ++i; }
                else if (u == "HS") { rr.rclass = rrclass::HS; ++i; }
                else if (!u.empty() && u[0] >= '0' && u[0] <= '9') { ttl = parseTtl(u); ++i; }
            }
            if (i >= tok.size()) throw fail("missing type");
            auto type = typeFromText(tok[i].text);
            if (!type) throw fail("unknown type " + tok[i].text);
            rr.type = *type;
            ++i;

            // TTL: explicit, else $TTL (RFC 2308 §4), else last explicit TTL (RFC 1035 §5.1)
            if (ttl) lastTtl = ttl;
            rr.ttl = ttl ? *ttl : ttlDirective ? *ttlDirective : lastTtl ? *lastTtl : defaultTtl;

            std::vector<ZoneToken> fields(tok.begin() + long(i), tok.end());
            rr.rdata = parseRdataText(rr.type, fields, current);
            zone.add(rr);
            lastOwner = rr.name;
        } catch (const ParseError& e) {
            std::string what = e.what();
            if (what.starts_with("line ")) throw;
            throw fail(what);
        }
    }
    zone.validate();
    return zone;
}

}  // namespace dns
```

**Test zone** `example.com.zone` (shown in full in §10.4). Loading it creates 18 records, the ENTs `b.c` and `c`, and rejects any file with syntax errors, a missing SOA, or CNAME-and-other-data. Try breaking it on purpose:

```bash
printf '$ORIGIN example.com.\n@ IN SOA ns1 h 1 2 3 4 5\n@ IN NS ns1\nx IN CNAME a\nx IN A 192.0.2.1\n' > bad.zone
./dnsd -z example.com. bad.zone
# error: line 5: CNAME and other data at x.example.com.
```

### 17.6 Stage 5 — name compression in responses

**Goal:** smaller responses. This needs no new code. `WireWriter::name()` in `wire.hpp` already keeps the suffix table (§5.5), `writeRdata()` compresses names inside NS/CNAME/SOA/PTR/MX RDATA only (§5.3), and `truncate()` forgets offsets past a rollback point.

**Test:** the `www.example.com A` response is 49 bytes with compression, against 64 bytes with the owner written in full. Count with `dig +stats` ("MSG SIZE rcvd: 49"), or compare the §4.6 hex dump. A wrong pointer shows up at once in `dig` as "malformed packet" or a garbled name. Wireshark marks it too.

### 17.7 Stage 6 — the full authoritative lookup

**Goal:** RFC 1034 §4.3.2 with every refinement from §11: zone selection, referrals with mandatory glue, CNAME chasing inside the zone, DNAME synthesis, wildcards, NXDOMAIN vs NODATA with SOA, ENTs, additional-section processing, and RFC 8482 for ANY.

`include/dns/authority.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <dns/zone.hpp>

namespace dns {

// Authoritative query processing (RFC 1034 §4.3.2, RFC 2308, RFC 4592, RFC 6672, RFC 8482).
class AuthoritativeServer {
public:
    void addZone(Zone z) { zones_.push_back(std::move(z)); }

    Message answer(const Message& query) const {
        Message resp;
        resp.header.id = query.header.id;
        resp.header.qr = true;
        resp.header.opcode = query.header.opcode;
        resp.header.rd = query.header.rd;  // RFC 1035 §4.1.1: RD is copied; CD stays clear (RFC 4035 §3.1.6)
        resp.questions = query.questions;
        if (query.edns) {
            resp.edns = Edns{};
            resp.edns->udpSize = kOurUdpSize;
            if (query.edns->version != 0) {  // RFC 6891 §6.1.3
                resp.setRcode(rcode::BADVERS);
                return resp;
            }
        }
        if (query.header.opcode != opcode::QUERY) {
            resp.setRcode(rcode::NOTIMP);
            return resp;
        }
        if (query.questions.size() != 1) {
            resp.setRcode(rcode::FORMERR);
            return resp;
        }
        const Question& q = query.questions[0];
        if (q.qclass != rrclass::IN || q.qtype == rrtype::AXFR || q.qtype == rrtype::IXFR) {
            resp.setRcode(rcode::REFUSED);
            return resp;
        }
        const Zone* zone = findZone(q.qname);
        if (!zone) {  // not authoritative and not a resolver
            resp.setRcode(rcode::REFUSED);
            return resp;
        }
        resp.header.aa = true;
        lookup(*zone, q.qname, q.qtype, resp);
        return resp;
    }

    static constexpr uint16_t kOurUdpSize = 1232;

private:
    // The zone with the longest origin that contains qname (RFC 1034 §4.3.2 step 2).
    const Zone* findZone(const DnsName& qname) const {
        const Zone* best = nullptr;
        for (const auto& z : zones_)
            if (qname.isSubdomainOf(z.origin()) && (!best || z.origin().labelCount() > best->origin().labelCount()))
                best = &z;
        return best;
    }

    static void emit(std::vector<ResourceRecord>& section, const DnsName& owner, const RRset& set) {
        for (const auto& rd : set.rdatas) section.push_back({owner, set.type, set.rclass, set.ttl, rd});
    }

    // Negative answers carry the zone's SOA; its TTL is min(SOA TTL, SOA MINIMUM) (RFC 2308 §3).
    static void addSoa(const Zone& zone, Message& resp) {
        const RRset& soa = *zone.find(zone.origin())->find(rrtype::SOA);
        ResourceRecord rr{zone.origin(), rrtype::SOA, soa.rclass, soa.ttl, soa.rdatas[0]};
        uint32_t minimum = uint32_t(rr.rdata[rr.rdata.size() - 4]) << 24 | uint32_t(rr.rdata[rr.rdata.size() - 3]) << 16 |
                           uint32_t(rr.rdata[rr.rdata.size() - 2]) << 8 | rr.rdata[rr.rdata.size() - 1];
        rr.ttl = std::min(rr.ttl, minimum);
        resp.authority.push_back(std::move(rr));
    }

    // A/AAAA for a target name, if this zone holds them. Glue below a zone cut is found too,
    // because Zone::find does not stop at delegations.
    static void addAddresses(const Zone& zone, const DnsName& target, Message& resp) {
        if (!target.isSubdomainOf(zone.origin())) return;
        const Node* node = zone.find(target);
        if (!node) return;
        for (uint16_t t : {rrtype::A, rrtype::AAAA})
            if (const RRset* set = node->find(t)) {
                bool dup = std::any_of(resp.additional.begin(), resp.additional.end(),
                                       [&](const ResourceRecord& r) { return r.type == t && r.name == target; });
                if (!dup) emit(resp.additional, target, *set);
            }
    }

    // Additional section processing for NS, MX and SRV targets (RFC 1035 §3.3.9, §3.3.11; RFC 2782).
    static void addAdditionalFor(const Zone& zone, const ResourceRecord& rr, Message& resp) {
        size_t off;
        switch (rr.type) {
        case rrtype::NS: off = 0; break;
        case rrtype::MX: off = 2; break;
        case rrtype::SRV: off = 6; break;
        default: return;
        }
        addAddresses(zone, DnsName::fromWire(std::span(rr.rdata).subspan(off)), resp);
    }

    static void referral(const Zone& zone, const DnsName& cut, const RRset& ns, Message& resp) {
        resp.header.aa = resp.answers.empty() ? false : resp.header.aa;  // AA describes the first owner only
        emit(resp.authority, cut, ns);
        if (const RRset* ds = zone.find(cut)->find(rrtype::DS)) emit(resp.authority, cut, *ds);
        // Glue for name servers inside the delegated zone is mandatory, sibling glue is not
        // (RFC 9471 §3.1, §3.2). Put the mandatory part first and tell the serialiser.
        std::vector<DnsName> targets;
        for (const auto& rd : ns.rdatas) targets.push_back(DnsName::fromWire(rd));
        for (const auto& t : targets)
            if (t.isSubdomainOf(cut)) addAddresses(zone, t, resp);
        resp.requiredAdditional = resp.additional.size();
        for (const auto& t : targets)
            if (!t.isSubdomainOf(cut)) addAddresses(zone, t, resp);
    }

    void lookup(const Zone& zone, DnsName qname, uint16_t qtype, Message& resp) const {
        for (int hops = 0; hops < kMaxChain; ++hops) {
            // Step 3b: walk down from the apex looking for a zone cut or a DNAME above qname.
            bool restart = false;
            for (size_t n = zone.origin().labelCount() + 1; n <= qname.labelCount(); ++n) {
                DnsName ancestor = qname.suffix(n);
                const Node* node = zone.find(ancestor);
                if (!node) break;  // nothing exists below here: handled as NXDOMAIN/wildcard
                bool atQname = n == qname.labelCount();
                if (const RRset* ns = node->find(rrtype::NS); ns && !(atQname && qtype == rrtype::DS)) {
                    referral(zone, ancestor, *ns, resp);
                    return;
                }
                if (const RRset* dname = node->find(rrtype::DNAME); dname && !atQname) {
                    // RFC 6672 §2.2: replace the DNAME owner suffix of qname with the target
                    DnsName target = DnsName::fromWire(dname->rdatas[0]);
                    DnsName synthesized = target;
                    try {
                        for (size_t k = qname.labelCount() - n; k-- > 0;)
                            synthesized = synthesized.withPrefix(qname.labels()[k]);
                    } catch (const ParseError&) {
                        emit(resp.answers, ancestor, *dname);
                        resp.setRcode(rcode::YXDOMAIN);  // result would exceed 255 octets
                        return;
                    }
                    emit(resp.answers, ancestor, *dname);
                    WireWriter w;
                    w.name(synthesized, false);
                    resp.answers.push_back({qname, rrtype::CNAME, dname->rclass, dname->ttl, w.data()});
                    if (!synthesized.isSubdomainOf(zone.origin())) return;  // resolver follows it
                    qname = synthesized;
                    restart = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (restart) continue;

            // Step 3a: exact match, else step 3c: wildcard at the closest encloser.
            const Node* node = zone.find(qname);
            if (!node) {
                DnsName encloser = qname.parent();
                while (!zone.find(encloser)) encloser = encloser.parent();  // stops at the apex at the latest
                node = zone.find(encloser.withPrefix("*"));
                if (!node) {
                    resp.setRcode(rcode::NXDOMAIN);  // RFC 6604: RCODE describes the last name in a chain
                    addSoa(zone, resp);
                    return;
                }
            }

            if (qtype == rrtype::ANY) {  // RFC 8482 §4.1: one RRset is a complete answer
                if (node->rrsets.empty()) { addSoa(zone, resp); return; }
                emit(resp.answers, qname, node->rrsets.begin()->second);
                return;
            }
            if (const RRset* set = node->find(qtype)) {
                emit(resp.answers, qname, *set);  // owner = qname: wildcard expansion (RFC 4592 §3.3.1)
                for (size_t i = resp.answers.size() - set->rdatas.size(); i < resp.answers.size(); ++i)
                    addAdditionalFor(zone, resp.answers[i], resp);
                return;
            }
            if (const RRset* cname = node->find(rrtype::CNAME); cname && qtype != rrtype::CNAME) {
                emit(resp.answers, qname, *cname);
                DnsName target = DnsName::fromWire(cname->rdatas[0]);
                if (!target.isSubdomainOf(zone.origin())) return;  // out of zone: resolver follows it
                qname = target;
                continue;
            }
            addSoa(zone, resp);  // NODATA: name exists, type does not (RFC 2308 §2.2)
            return;
        }
        // Chain longer than kMaxChain: return what we have; the resolver will give up or retry.
    }

    static constexpr int kMaxChain = 16;
    std::vector<Zone> zones_;
};

}  // namespace dns
```

**Test**, with the §10.4 zone loaded. Each line was checked against the running server, and the hex dumps in §11 are its real output:

```bash
d() { dig @127.0.0.1 -p 5353 +norec "$@"; }
d www.example.com A           # AA, 192.0.2.10
d web.example.com A           # CNAME web → www, then the A record, both in the answer
d nope.example.com A          # NXDOMAIN, SOA in authority with TTL 300
d www.example.com MX          # NOERROR, empty answer, SOA (NODATA)
d b.c.example.com TXT         # NODATA, not NXDOMAIN: empty non-terminal (RFC 8020)
d x.wild.example.com A        # 192.0.2.99 with owner x.wild.example.com (wildcard)
d host.sub.example.com A      # no AA, authority: sub NS ns.sub, additional: glue 192.0.2.200
d x.legacy.example.com A      # DNAME + synthesised CNAME to x.example.net
d example.com MX              # MX 10 mail + additional mail A 192.0.2.25
d example.org A               # REFUSED
```

### 17.8 Stage 7 — TCP support and truncation

**Goal:** serve TCP on the same port: length-prefixed messages, pipelining, partial reads and writes, idle timeouts, connection limits (§8.4–8.5). Also truncate UDP responses correctly, with TC=1 and nothing partial (§8.3). The truncation logic is `serializeMessage()` in `message.hpp`. The TCP handling is in the server program below (stage 10 contains the full listing). `processTcpInput()` extracts complete messages from the buffer, so it handles split and pipelined input.

**Test:**

```bash
d www.example.com A +tcp                       # same answer over TCP
for i in $(seq 1 30); do echo "big IN TXT \"record number $i padding padding padding\""; done >> example.com.zone
# restart, then:
d big.example.com TXT +noedns +ignore          # tc flag set, ANSWER: 0, 33 bytes
d big.example.com TXT +noedns                  # dig retries over TCP automatically: 30 records
```

The 30 TXT records need 1,614 bytes. Even with EDNS 4096 the response is truncated, because the server caps UDP at its own 1,232 (§9.4).

### 17.9 Stage 8 — EDNS(0)

**Goal:** a correct minimal EDNS implementation (§9.6). Parse OPT, reject duplicates (FORMERR), answer version ≠ 0 with BADVERS, always include an OPT when the query had one, honour `min(client size, 1232)` and never go below 512. The code is spread over `parseOpt`/`writeOpt`/`serializeMessage` in `message.hpp`, the start of `AuthoritativeServer::answer()`, and the size limit in `handle()` in `server.cpp`.

**Test:**

```bash
d www.example.com A                     # "; EDNS: version: 0, flags:; udp: 1232" in the OPT PSEUDOSECTION
d www.example.com A +edns=1 +noednsneg  # status: BADVERS, EDNS version 0 in the reply
d www.example.com A +bufsize=512        # replies fit in 512
```

### 17.10 Stage 9 — a recursive resolver with a cache

**Goal:** answer RD=1 queries for names outside our zones by iterating from the root (§12), with a TTL-respecting cache, negative caching and trust ranking (§13), bailiwick filtering, random IDs, random source ports and 0x20 (§16).

`include/dns/resolver.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <dns/message.hpp>

#include <arpa/inet.h>
#include <netinet/in.h>
#include <poll.h>
#include <sys/random.h>
#include <sys/socket.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cstring>

namespace dns {

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- cache

// RFC 2181 §5.4.1: data from the answer section of an authoritative reply outranks
// referral NS sets and glue. Lower-ranked data never replaces higher-ranked data and is
// never handed to clients as an answer.
enum class Trust { Glue = 0, Answer = 1 };

class Cache {
public:
    using Clock = std::chrono::steady_clock;
    static constexpr uint32_t kMaxTtl = 86400;         // cap for positive data
    static constexpr uint32_t kMaxNegativeTtl = 10800;  // RFC 2308 §5 suggests 1-3 hours
    static constexpr uint16_t kNxdomainType = 0;        // pseudo type: "this name does not exist"

    struct Entry {
        std::vector<ResourceRecord> records;  // empty for negative entries
        bool negative = false;
        uint16_t rcode = rcode::NOERROR;
        std::optional<ResourceRecord> soa;  // proof for negative entries
        Trust trust = Trust::Glue;
        Clock::time_point stored, expires;
    };

    // Returns a copy with TTLs reduced by the time spent in the cache.
    std::optional<Entry> get(const DnsName& name, uint16_t type, Trust minTrust) {
        auto it = map_.find(key(name, type));
        if (it == map_.end()) return std::nullopt;
        auto now = Clock::now();
        if (now >= it->second.expires) {
            map_.erase(it);
            return std::nullopt;
        }
        if (it->second.trust < minTrust) return std::nullopt;
        Entry e = it->second;
        auto left = uint32_t(std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::seconds>(e.expires - now).count());
        for (auto& rr : e.records) rr.ttl = left;
        if (e.soa) e.soa->ttl = left;
        return e;
    }

    // Stores one RRset (all records must share name and type).
    void putRRset(std::vector<ResourceRecord> rrs, Trust trust) {
        if (rrs.empty()) return;
        uint32_t ttl = kMaxTtl;
        for (const auto& rr : rrs) ttl = std::min(ttl, rr.ttl);  // RFC 2181 §5.2
        for (auto& rr : rrs) rr.ttl = ttl;
        std::string k = key(rrs[0].name, rrs[0].type);
        if (auto it = map_.find(k); it != map_.end() && it->second.trust > trust && Clock::now() < it->second.expires)
            return;
        Entry e;
        e.records = std::move(rrs);
        e.trust = trust;
        e.stored = Clock::now();
        e.expires = e.stored + std::chrono::seconds(ttl);
        map_[k] = std::move(e);
    }

    // RFC 2308 §5: negative TTL = min(SOA TTL, SOA MINIMUM). NXDOMAIN covers every type.
    void putNegative(const DnsName& name, uint16_t type, uint16_t rc, const ResourceRecord& soa) {
        const auto& rd = soa.rdata;
        uint32_t minimum = uint32_t(rd[rd.size() - 4]) << 24 | uint32_t(rd[rd.size() - 3]) << 16 |
                           uint32_t(rd[rd.size() - 2]) << 8 | rd[rd.size() - 1];
        uint32_t ttl = std::min({soa.ttl, minimum, kMaxNegativeTtl});
        Entry e;
        e.negative = true;
        e.rcode = rc;
        e.soa = soa;
        e.trust = Trust::Answer;
        e.stored = Clock::now();
        e.expires = e.stored + std::chrono::seconds(ttl);
        map_[key(name, rc == rcode::NXDOMAIN ? kNxdomainType : type)] = std::move(e);
    }

private:
    static std::string key(const DnsName& n, uint16_t type) {
        return n.key() + char(type >> 8) + char(type & 0xFF);
    }
    std::unordered_map<std::string, Entry> map_;
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- transport helpers

inline uint16_t random16() {
    uint16_t v;
    if (getrandom(&v, sizeof v, 0) != sizeof v) throw std::runtime_error("getrandom failed");
    return v;
}

// DNS 0x20 (draft-vixie-dnsext-dns0x20): randomise the case of every letter; a genuine
// reply echoes the question byte-for-byte, an off-path forger has to guess the pattern.
inline DnsName randomizeCase(const DnsName& n) {
    DnsName out;
    for (std::string label : n.labels()) {
        for (char& c : label) {
            char lower = asciiLower(c);
            if (lower >= 'a' && lower <= 'z') c = (random16() & 1) ? char(lower - 32) : lower;
        }
        out.appendLabel(std::move(label));
    }
    return out;
}

inline bool exactSameName(const DnsName& a, const DnsName& b) { return a.labels() == b.labels(); }

inline bool readFull(int fd, uint8_t* p, size_t n) {
    while (n > 0) {
        ssize_t got = recv(fd, p, n, 0);
        if (got <= 0) return false;
        p += got;
        n -= size_t(got);
    }
    return true;
}

// One query over TCP with the 2-byte length prefix (RFC 1035 §4.2.2, RFC 7766 §8).
inline std::optional<std::vector<uint8_t>> exchangeTcp(const sockaddr_in& server, const std::vector<uint8_t>& query,
                                                       int timeoutMs) {
    int fd = socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, 0);
    if (fd < 0) return std::nullopt;
    timeval tv{timeoutMs / 1000, (timeoutMs % 1000) * 1000};
    setsockopt(fd, SOL_SOCKET, SO_RCVTIMEO, &tv, sizeof tv);
    setsockopt(fd, SOL_SOCKET, SO_SNDTIMEO, &tv, sizeof tv);
    std::optional<std::vector<uint8_t>> result;
    if (connect(fd, reinterpret_cast<const sockaddr*>(&server), sizeof server) == 0) {
        std::vector<uint8_t> framed{uint8_t(query.size() >> 8), uint8_t(query.size())};
        framed.insert(framed.end(), query.begin(), query.end());
        uint8_t lenBuf[2];
        if (send(fd, framed.data(), framed.size(), MSG_NOSIGNAL) == ssize_t(framed.size()) && readFull(fd, lenBuf, 2)) {
            std::vector<uint8_t> resp(size_t(lenBuf[0] << 8 | lenBuf[1]));
            if (readFull(fd, resp.data(), resp.size())) result = std::move(resp);
        }
    }
    close(fd);
    return result;
}

// One query over UDP from a fresh socket. Binding to port 0 lets the kernel pick a random
// ephemeral source port; connect() makes the kernel drop datagrams from any other address/port.
inline std::optional<std::vector<uint8_t>> exchangeUdp(const sockaddr_in& server, const std::vector<uint8_t>& query,
                                                       uint16_t id, int timeoutMs) {
    int fd = socket(AF_INET, SOCK_DGRAM, 0);
    if (fd < 0) return std::nullopt;
    std::optional<std::vector<uint8_t>> result;
    if (connect(fd, reinterpret_cast<const sockaddr*>(&server), sizeof server) == 0 &&
        send(fd, query.data(), query.size(), 0) == ssize_t(query.size())) {
        auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::milliseconds(timeoutMs);
        std::vector<uint8_t> buf(65535);
        for (;;) {
            auto left = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(deadline - std::chrono::steady_clock::now());
            pollfd p{fd, POLLIN, 0};
            if (left.count() <= 0 || poll(&p, 1, int(left.count())) <= 0) break;
            ssize_t n = recv(fd, buf.data(), buf.size(), 0);
            if (n >= 2 && (buf[0] << 8 | buf[1]) == id) {  // ignore datagrams with the wrong ID
                buf.resize(size_t(n));
                result = std::move(buf);
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    close(fd);
    return result;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- iterative resolver

struct Resolution {
    uint16_t rcode = rcode::SERVFAIL;
    std::vector<ResourceRecord> answers;  // CNAME chain + final RRset
    std::optional<ResourceRecord> soa;    // for negative answers
};

class Resolver {
public:
    Resolution resolve(DnsName qname, uint16_t qtype, int depth = 0) {
        Resolution res;
        if (depth > kMaxDepth) return res;
        for (int hop = 0; hop < kMaxCnameHops; ++hop) {
            if (auto e = cache_.get(qname, Cache::kNxdomainType, Trust::Answer)) {
                res.rcode = e->rcode;
                res.soa = e->soa;
                return res;
            }
            if (auto e = cache_.get(qname, qtype, Trust::Answer)) {
                res.rcode = e->negative ? e->rcode : rcode::NOERROR;
                res.soa = e->soa;
                res.answers.insert(res.answers.end(), e->records.begin(), e->records.end());
                return res;
            }
            if (qtype != rrtype::CNAME)
                if (auto e = cache_.get(qname, rrtype::CNAME, Trust::Answer); e && !e->negative) {
                    res.answers.push_back(e->records[0]);
                    qname = DnsName::fromWire(e->records[0].rdata);
                    continue;
                }

            auto step = iterate(qname, qtype, depth);
            if (!step) return res;  // SERVFAIL
            res.answers.insert(res.answers.end(), step->records.begin(), step->records.end());
            if (step->kind == Step::Cname) {
                qname = step->next;  // chain leaves the zone: start again from the cache/root
                continue;
            }
            res.rcode = step->rcode;
            res.soa = step->soa;
            return res;
        }
        return res;  // CNAME chain too long: SERVFAIL
    }

private:
    static constexpr int kMaxDepth = 6;        // nested lookups for name server addresses
    static constexpr int kMaxCnameHops = 12;
    static constexpr int kMaxReferrals = 24;
    static constexpr int kTimeoutMs = 1500;

    static std::vector<sockaddr_in> rootHints() {
        // a..m.root-servers.net IPv4 addresses; refresh from https://www.internic.net/domain/named.root
        static const char* ips[] = {"198.41.0.4",     "170.247.170.2", "192.33.4.12",   "199.7.91.13",  "192.203.230.10",
                                    "192.5.5.241",    "192.112.36.4",  "198.97.190.53", "192.36.148.17", "192.58.128.30",
                                    "193.0.14.129",   "199.7.83.42",   "202.12.27.33"};
        std::vector<sockaddr_in> v;
        for (const char* ip : ips) v.push_back(endpoint(ip));
        return v;
    }

    static sockaddr_in endpoint(const char* ip) {
        sockaddr_in a{};
        a.sin_family = AF_INET;
        a.sin_port = htons(53);
        inet_pton(AF_INET, ip, &a.sin_addr);
        return a;
    }

    // Sends one iterative query and validates the reply (ID, QR, question echo incl. 0x20 case).
    std::optional<Message> query(const sockaddr_in& server, const DnsName& qname, uint16_t qtype) {
        Message q;
        q.header.id = random16();
```
